+    private static void RegisterArithmeticFamily(ICollection<CilIntrinsicDescriptor> descriptors)
+    {
+        var operations = new[] { "add", "sub", "mul", "div" };
+        var operandTypes = new[] { "i32", "i64", "f32", "f64", "decimal" };
+
+        foreach (var operation in operations)
+        foreach (var operandType in operandTypes)
+        {
+            var name = $"{operation}_{operandType}";
+            descriptors.Add(CreateDescriptor(
+                name,
+                static (context, instruction, stack) => CompileArithmeticIntrinsic(context, instruction.Operands[0].Get<string>(), stack)));
+        }
+    }
+
+    private static void RegisterComparisonFamily(ICollection<CilIntrinsicDescriptor> descriptors)
+    {
+        var operations = new[] { "eq", "ne", "gt", "ge", "lt", "le" };
+        var operandTypes = new[] { "i32", "i64", "f32", "f64" };
+
+        foreach (var operation in operations)
+        foreach (var operandType in operandTypes)
+        {
+            var name = $"cmp_{operation}_{operandType}";
+            descriptors.Add(CreateDescriptor(
+                name,
+                static (context, instruction, stack) => CompileComparisonIntrinsic(context, instruction.Operands[0].Get<string>(), stack)));
+        }
+    }

+namespace BytecodeDynamicMethodsCompiler.Compilers;
+
+internal sealed class CilIntrinsicRegistry
+{
+    private readonly IReadOnlyDictionary<string, CilIntrinsicDescriptor> _descriptors;
+
+    public CilIntrinsicRegistry(IEnumerable<CilIntrinsicDescriptor> descriptors)
+    {
+        var orderedDescriptors = descriptors
+            .OrderBy(descriptor => descriptor.Name, StringComparer.Ordinal)
+            .ToArray();
+
+        var registeredDescriptors = new Dictionary<string, CilIntrinsicDescriptor>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
+        foreach (var descriptor in orderedDescriptors)
+        {
+            if (!registeredDescriptors.TryAdd(descriptor.Name, descriptor))
+                Thrower.InvalidOpEx($"Duplicate CIL intrinsic registration: {descriptor.Name}");
+        }
+
+        _descriptors = registeredDescriptors;
+        SupportedIntrinsics = orderedDescriptors.Select(descriptor => descriptor.Name).ToArray();
+    }
+
+    public IReadOnlyList<string> SupportedIntrinsics { get; }
+
+    public bool TryGet(string name, out CilIntrinsicDescriptor descriptor)
+        => _descriptors.TryGetValue(name, out descriptor!);
+
+    public CilIntrinsicDescriptor GetRequired(string name)
+        => TryGet(name, out var descriptor)
+            ? descriptor
+            : Thrower.InvalidOpEx<CilIntrinsicDescriptor>($"Unsupported intrinsic: {name}");

+        {
+            CreateDescriptor("call C#", CompileCallCSharp),
+            CreateDescriptor("call C# ctor", CompileCallCSharpCtor),
+            CreateDescriptor("store_local", CompileStoreLocal),
+            CreateDescriptor("load_local", CompileLoadLocal),
+            CreateDescriptor("load_local_ref", CompileLoadLocalRef),
+            CreateDescriptor("load_external", CompileLoadExternal),
+            CreateDescriptor("store_external", CompileStoreExternal),
+            CreateDescriptor("load_bool", CompileLoadBool),
+            CreateDescriptor("boolean_and", CompileBooleanAnd),
+            CreateDescriptor("boolean_or", CompileBooleanOr),
+            CreateDescriptor("boolean_not", CompileBooleanNot),

+            CreateDescriptor("load_i64", LoadNativeNumber),
+            CreateDescriptor("load_f32", LoadNativeNumber),
+            CreateDescriptor("load_f64", LoadNativeNumber),
+            CreateDescriptor("load_decimal", LoadNativeNumber)

+

+        RegisterComparisonFamily(descriptors);
+

+    private static CilIntrinsicDescriptor CreateDescriptor(
+        string name,
+        Action<CompilationContext, Instruction, List<Type>> compile,
+        Action<Instruction, List<Type>>? processTypes = null)
+        => new(name, compile, processTypes ?? NoTypeProcessing);
+
+    private static void RegisterArithmeticFamily(ICollection<CilIntrinsicDescriptor> descriptors)
+    {
+        var operations = new[] { "add", "sub", "mul", "div" };
+        var operandTypes = new[] { "i32", "i64", "f32", "f64", "decimal" };
+
+        foreach (var operation in operations)
+        foreach (var operandType in operandTypes)
+        {
+            var name = $"{operation}_{operandType}";
+            descriptors.Add(CreateDescriptor(
+                name,
+                static (context, instruction, stack) => CompileArithmeticIntrinsic(context, instruction.Operands[0].Get<string>(), stack)));
+        }
+    }
+
+    private static void RegisterComparisonFamily(ICollection<CilIntrinsicDescriptor> descriptors)
+    {
+        var operations = new[] { "eq", "ne", "gt", "ge", "lt", "le" };
+        var operandTypes = new[] { "i32", "i64", "f32", "f64" };
+
+        foreach (var operation in operations)
+        foreach (var operandType in operandTypes)
+        {
+            var name = $"cmp_{operation}_{operandType}";
+            descriptors.Add(CreateDescriptor(
+                name,
+                static (context, instruction, stack) => CompileComparisonIntrinsic(context, instruction.Operands[0].Get<string>(), stack)));
+        }
+    }

diff --git a/UniversalToolchain/BytecodeDynamicMethodsCompiler/Compilers/CilIntrinsicRegistry.cs b/UniversalToolchain/BytecodeDynamicMethodsCompiler/Compilers/CilIntrinsicRegistry.cs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..120bf011270a57a7d5f4816e8675ed6812fd71b6
--- /dev/null
+++ b/UniversalToolchain/BytecodeDynamicMethodsCompiler/Compilers/CilIntrinsicRegistry.cs
@@ -0,0 +1,33 @@
+namespace BytecodeDynamicMethodsCompiler.Compilers;
+
+internal sealed class CilIntrinsicRegistry
+{
+    private readonly IReadOnlyDictionary<string, CilIntrinsicDescriptor> _descriptors;
+
+    public CilIntrinsicRegistry(IEnumerable<CilIntrinsicDescriptor> descriptors)
+    {
+        var orderedDescriptors = descriptors
+            .OrderBy(descriptor => descriptor.Name, StringComparer.Ordinal)
+            .ToArray();
+
+        var registeredDescriptors = new Dictionary<string, CilIntrinsicDescriptor>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
+        foreach (var descriptor in orderedDescriptors)
+        {
+            if (!registeredDescriptors.TryAdd(descriptor.Name, descriptor))
+                Thrower.InvalidOpEx($"Duplicate CIL intrinsic registration: {descriptor.Name}");
+        }
+
+        _descriptors = registeredDescriptors;
+        SupportedIntrinsics = orderedDescriptors.Select(descriptor => descriptor.Name).ToArray();
+    }
+
+    public IReadOnlyList<string> SupportedIntrinsics { get; }
+
+    public bool TryGet(string name, out CilIntrinsicDescriptor descriptor)
+        => _descriptors.TryGetValue(name, out descriptor!);
+
+    public CilIntrinsicDescriptor GetRequired(string name)
+        => TryGet(name, out var descriptor)
+            ? descriptor
+            : Thrower.InvalidOpEx<CilIntrinsicDescriptor>($"Unsupported intrinsic: {name}");
+}

+    private static void RegisterArithmeticFamily(ICollection<CilIntrinsicDescriptor> descriptors)
+    {
+        var operations = new[] { "add", "sub", "mul", "div" };
+        var operandTypes = new[] { "i32", "i64", "f32", "f64", "decimal" };
+
+        foreach (var operation in operations)
+        foreach (var operandType in operandTypes)
+        {
+            var name = $"{operation}_{operandType}";
+            descriptors.Add(CreateDescriptor(
+                name,
+                static (context, instruction, stack) => CompileArithmeticIntrinsic(context, instruction.Operands[0].Get<string>(), stack)));
+        }
+    }
+
+    private static void RegisterComparisonFamily(ICollection<CilIntrinsicDescriptor> descriptors)
+    {
+        var operations = new[] { "eq", "ne", "gt", "ge", "lt", "le" };
+        var operandTypes = new[] { "i32", "i64", "f32", "f64" };
+
+        foreach (var operation in operations)
+        foreach (var operandType in operandTypes)
+        {
+            var name = $"cmp_{operation}_{operandType}";
+            descriptors.Add(CreateDescriptor(
+                name,
+                static (context, instruction, stack) => CompileComparisonIntrinsic(context, instruction.Operands[0].Get<string>(), stack)));
+        }
+    }

diff --git a/UniversalToolchain/BytecodeDynamicMethodsCompiler/Compilers/CilIntrinsicRegistry.cs b/UniversalToolchain/BytecodeDynamicMethodsCompiler/Compilers/CilIntrinsicRegistry.cs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..120bf011270a57a7d5f4816e8675ed6812fd71b6
--- /dev/null
+++ b/UniversalToolchain/BytecodeDynamicMethodsCompiler/Compilers/CilIntrinsicRegistry.cs
@@ -0,0 +1,33 @@
+namespace BytecodeDynamicMethodsCompiler.Compilers;
+
+internal sealed class CilIntrinsicRegistry
+{
+    private readonly IReadOnlyDictionary<string, CilIntrinsicDescriptor> _descriptors;
+
+    public CilIntrinsicRegistry(IEnumerable<CilIntrinsicDescriptor> descriptors)
+    {
+        var orderedDescriptors = descriptors
+            .OrderBy(descriptor => descriptor.Name, StringComparer.Ordinal)
+            .ToArray();
+
+        var registeredDescriptors = new Dictionary<string, CilIntrinsicDescriptor>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
+        foreach (var descriptor in orderedDescriptors)
+        {
+            if (!registeredDescriptors.TryAdd(descriptor.Name, descriptor))
+                Thrower.InvalidOpEx($"Duplicate CIL intrinsic registration: {descriptor.Name}");
+        }
+
+        _descriptors = registeredDescriptors;
+        SupportedIntrinsics = orderedDescriptors.Select(descriptor => descriptor.Name).ToArray();
+    }
+
+    public IReadOnlyList<string> SupportedIntrinsics { get; }
+
+    public bool TryGet(string name, out CilIntrinsicDescriptor descriptor)
+        => _descriptors.TryGetValue(name, out descriptor!);
+
+    public CilIntrinsicDescriptor GetRequired(string name)
+        => TryGet(name, out var descriptor)
+            ? descriptor
+            : Thrower.InvalidOpEx<CilIntrinsicDescriptor>($"Unsupported intrinsic: {name}");
+}

+        foreach (var operandType in operandTypes)
+        {
+            var name = $"{operation}_{operandType}";
+            descriptors.Add(CreateDescriptor(
+                name,
+                static (context, instruction, stack) => CompileArithmeticIntrinsic(context, instruction.Operands[0].Get<string>(), stack)));
+        }
+    }
+
+    private static void RegisterComparisonFamily(ICollection<CilIntrinsicDescriptor> descriptors)
+    {
+        var operations = new[] { "eq", "ne", "gt", "ge", "lt", "le" };
+        var operandTypes = new[] { "i32", "i64", "f32", "f64" };
+
+        foreach (var operation in operations)
+        foreach (var operandType in operandTypes)
+        {
+            var name = $"cmp_{operation}_{operandType}";
+            descriptors.Add(CreateDescriptor(
+                name,
+                static (context, instruction, stack) => CompileComparisonIntrinsic(context, instruction.Operands[0].Get<string>(), stack)));
+        }
+    }
 }

diff --git a/UniversalToolchain/BytecodeDynamicMethodsCompiler/Compilers/CilIntrinsicRegistry.cs b/UniversalToolchain/BytecodeDynamicMethodsCompiler/Compilers/CilIntrinsicRegistry.cs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..120bf011270a57a7d5f4816e8675ed6812fd71b6
--- /dev/null
+++ b/UniversalToolchain/BytecodeDynamicMethodsCompiler/Compilers/CilIntrinsicRegistry.cs
@@ -0,0 +1,33 @@
+namespace BytecodeDynamicMethodsCompiler.Compilers;
+
+internal sealed class CilIntrinsicRegistry
+{
+    private readonly IReadOnlyDictionary<string, CilIntrinsicDescriptor> _descriptors;
+
+    public CilIntrinsicRegistry(IEnumerable<CilIntrinsicDescriptor> descriptors)
+    {
+        var orderedDescriptors = descriptors
+            .OrderBy(descriptor => descriptor.Name, StringComparer.Ordinal)
+            .ToArray();
+
+        var registeredDescriptors = new Dictionary<string, CilIntrinsicDescriptor>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
+        foreach (var descriptor in orderedDescriptors)
+        {
+            if (!registeredDescriptors.TryAdd(descriptor.Name, descriptor))
+                Thrower.InvalidOpEx($"Duplicate CIL intrinsic registration: {descriptor.Name}");
+        }
+
+        _descriptors = registeredDescriptors;
+        SupportedIntrinsics = orderedDescriptors.Select(descriptor => descriptor.Name).ToArray();
+    }
+
+    public IReadOnlyList<string> SupportedIntrinsics { get; }
+
+    public bool TryGet(string name, out CilIntrinsicDescriptor descriptor)
+        => _descriptors.TryGetValue(name, out descriptor!);
+
+    public CilIntrinsicDescriptor GetRequired(string name)
+        => TryGet(name, out var descriptor)
+            ? descriptor
+            : Thrower.InvalidOpEx<CilIntrinsicDescriptor>($"Unsupported intrinsic: {name}");
+}

codex
Implemented an explicit CIL intrinsic registry so the backend now has one source of truth for intrinsic compile semantics and supported intrinsic names. `AbstractMethodsIntrinsicCompiler` is now a thin executor over registry descriptors, and arithmetic/comparison families are registered through the registry instead of being inferred from separate hardcoded lists.

Files changed:
- [AbstractMethodsIntrinsicCompiler.cs](/home/micodiy/razakov/sharp/Wist2/UniversalToolchain/BytecodeDynamicMethodsCompiler/Compilers/AbstractMethodsIntrinsicCompiler.cs)
- [CilIntrinsicDescriptor.cs](/home/micodiy/razakov/sharp/Wist2/UniversalToolchain/BytecodeDynamicMethodsCompiler/Compilers/CilIntrinsicDescriptor.cs)
- [CilIntrinsicRegistry.cs](/home/micodiy/razakov/sharp/Wist2/UniversalToolchain/BytecodeDynamicMethodsCompiler/Compilers/CilIntrinsicRegistry.cs)
- [CilBackendAbstractIrCompilationTests.cs](/home/micodiy/razakov/sharp/Wist2/UniversalToolchain/Tests/Backends/CilBackendAbstractIrCompilationTests.cs)

Validated:
- `dotnet build UniversalToolchain/Wist.sln -c Release -maxcpucount:1 -nodeReuse:false` passed.
- Added a test asserting `SupportedIntrinsics` is unique, sorted, and still includes representative registered intrinsics/families.
- `dotnet test` could not run in this sandbox because `vstest` failed on a socket permission error.

Remaining risk or uncertainty:
- `ProcessTypes` is intentionally present but still wired as a placeholder on descriptors, since migrating AIR type simulation was explicitly out of scope for this commit.
- The sandbox prevented executing the test suite, so runtime verification is limited to a successful compile plus code review of the registry wiring.

+    public IReadOnlyList<string> SupportedIntrinsics { get; }
+
+    public bool TryGet(string name, out CilIntrinsicDescriptor descriptor)
+        => _descriptors.TryGetValue(name, out descriptor!);
+
+    public CilIntrinsicDescriptor GetRequired(string name)
+        => TryGet(name, out var descriptor)
+            ? descriptor
+            : Thrower.InvalidOpEx<CilIntrinsicDescriptor>($"Unsupported intrinsic: {name}");
+}
